:

e^{\big<z^{\dagger}z\big>}\Bigg[(MM^{\dagger})^{2}\Big\{\big<\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}\big>-3\Big\}+m_{p}^{2}|M|^{2}a_{p}^{\ast}a^{p}+\Big(m_{p}M^{\dagger}a^{p}\big<\tilde{W}_{p}\big>+\text{h.c.}\Big)\Bigg]\ .

C.2
Case of a renormalisable theory

We now consider the most general renormalisable theory, for which we have the substitution:

\displaystyle\tilde{W}_{k}(z)g^{k}({\cal U},\Phi)

\displaystyle\to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}m_{ab}(z)\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}+\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{ab}(z){\cal U}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}+\frac{1}{6}y_{abc}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}\Phi^{c}+\frac{1}{6}\lambda(z){\cal U}^{3}+\frac{1}{2}\mu(z){\cal U}^{2}\ ,

\displaystyle Z_{\ell}(z,z^{\dagger})h^{\ell}(\Phi)

\displaystyle\to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}Z_{ab}(z,z^{\dagger})\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}\ .

We thus consider,

\displaystyle W(z,\mathcal{U},\Phi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{p}\big[\hat{W}_{0}(z)+S^{p}\mu^{\ast}_{p}\hat{W}_{1}(z)\big]+S^{p}\xi_{p}(z)+\frac{1}{2}m_{ab}(z)\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{ab}(z){\cal U}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}+\frac{1}{6}y_{abc}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}\Phi^{c}+\frac{1}{6}\lambda(z){\cal U}^{3}+\frac{1}{2}\mu(z){\cal U}^{2}\ ,

\displaystyle K(z,z^{\dagger},\Phi,\Phi^{\dagger},S,S^{\dagger})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{p}^{2}K(z,z^{\dagger})+S_{p}^{\dagger}S^{p}+\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}(z,z^{\dagger})\Phi^{\dagger}_{a^{\ast}}\Phi^{a}+\frac{1}{2}\big[Z_{ab}(z,z^{\dagger})\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}+\text{h.c.}\big]

where we have denoted
\tilde{W}_{p}S^{p}=\xi_{p}S^{p}
. The potential is given by

\displaystyle V=\Lambda+V_{\text{SUSY}}+V_{\text{soft}}+V_{\text{hard}}\ ,

where
\Lambda
is the cosmological constant. The supersymmetric part of the potential reduces to

\displaystyle V_{\text{SUSY}}=\partial_{p}W^{\prime}_{m}\partial^{p}W^{\prime}{}^{\ast}_{m}+\partial_{a}W_{m}\partial^{a^{\ast}}W_{m}^{\ast}(\Lambda^{-1})^{a}{}_{a^{\ast}}\ ,

with the superpotential

\displaystyle W_{m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\hat{m}_{ab}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}+\frac{1}{6}\hat{y}_{abc}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}\Phi^{c}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{y}_{abp}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}S^{p}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\sigma}_{ab}{\cal U}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}

\displaystyle+\hat{\xi}_{p}S^{p}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\mu}{\cal U}^{2}+\frac{1}{6}\hat{\lambda}{\cal U}^{3}

and

\displaystyle W^{\prime}_{m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\Big(\frac{1}{2}m_{ab}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}+\frac{1}{6}y_{abc}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}\Phi^{c}+\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{ab}{\cal U}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}

\displaystyle+\xi_{p}S^{p}+\frac{1}{2}\mu{\cal U}^{2}+\frac{1}{6}\lambda{\cal U}^{3}\Big)\ .

We have introduced the notations:

\displaystyle\hat{m}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}m_{ab}+m_{\frac{3}{2}}\big[Z_{ab}-\rho_{i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{ab}\big]\ ,

\displaystyle\hat{y}_{abc}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}y_{abc}\ ,

\displaystyle\hat{y}_{abp}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M}\big[Z_{ab}-\rho_{1i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{ab}\big]a^{\ast}_{p}\ ,

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\sigma_{ab}\ ,

\displaystyle\hat{\xi}_{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\xi_{p}\ ,

\displaystyle\hat{\mu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\mu\ ,

\displaystyle\hat{\lambda}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\lambda\ .

The supersymmetric breaking terms are of two types: soft supersymmetric breaking terms (as usual) and hard supersymmetric breaking terms, controlled by
the chiral superfields
S^{1}
and
S^{2}
. These terms are expressed with respect to the two functions

\displaystyle\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{i}\Big(\hat{K}+\ln\frac{\hat{W}}{m_{p}^{2}}\Big)(K^{-1})^{i}{}_{i^{\ast}}\ \ ,\ \ \hat{W}=\hat{W}_{0}+S^{p}\mu^{\ast}_{p}\hat{W}_{1}\ ,

\displaystyle\hat{\rho}_{1}{}_{i^{\ast}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{i}\Big(\hat{K}+\ln\frac{\hat{W}_{1}}{m_{p}^{2}}\Big)(K^{-1})^{i}{}_{i^{\ast}}\ .

We also recall that the mass scale are defined by

\displaystyle M^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big<\hat{W}_{0}+\mu^{\ast}_{p}S^{p}\hat{W}_{1}\Big>\ ,

\displaystyle a^{\ast}_{p}M

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu^{\ast}_{p}\big<\hat{W}_{1}\big>\ ,

\displaystyle m_{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\big<\hat{K}\big>}\frac{M^{2}}{m_{p}}\ .

From now on to easy the notation the symbol
\big<\cdots\big>
is omitted when the
z-
fields develop vacuum expectation values.
For instance
\hat{K}
is understood for
\big<\hat{K}\big>
. The soft supersymmetric breaking terms are

\displaystyle V_{\text{soft}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(m^{2})^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}\Phi^{\dagger}_{a^{\ast}}\Phi^{a}+(m^{2})^{p}{}_{q}S^{\dagger}_{p}S^{q}

\displaystyle+\Bigg\{m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\Big[\mu{\cal U}\partial_{p}{\cal U}+\frac{1}{2}\lambda{\cal U}^{2}\partial_{p}{\cal U}\Big]\Big[S^{p}+\langle S^{p}\rangle\Big]+\frac{m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M^{\dagger}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\mu{\cal U}\partial_{p}{\cal U}\langle S^{p}\rangle a^{q}S^{\dagger}_{q}+\text{h.c.}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\Bigg\{\frac{1}{6}A_{abc}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}\Phi^{c}+\frac{1}{2}B_{ab}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}+\frac{1}{2}B^{\prime}_{pq}S^{p}S^{q}+\frac{1}{2}A^{\prime}_{abp}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}S^{p}+\frac{1}{2}A^{\prime\prime}_{ab}{\cal U}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}

\displaystyle+C_{p}S^{p}+\frac{1}{2}B^{\prime\prime}{\cal U}^{2}+\frac{1}{6}D^{\prime}{\cal U}^{3}+\text{h.c.}\Bigg\}

with:

\displaystyle(m^{2})^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}\Bigg[\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}\Bigg\{\Bigg(\frac{\big<S^{p}\big>a^{\ast}_{p}}{M}+{\rm h.c.}\Bigg)+(\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-2)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\Big(\partial^{i^{\ast}}\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{b}(\Lambda^{-1})^{b}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{j}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}-\partial_{j}\partial^{i^{\ast}}\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}\Big)\hat{\rho}^{\ast j}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\Bigg(|M|^{2}a_{q}^{\ast}a^{q}e^{\hat{K}}+\Bigg\{\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}}{M^{\dagger}}a^{q}\hat{\xi}_{q}+{\rm h.c.}\Bigg\}\Bigg)\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}\ ,

\displaystyle(m^{2})^{p}{}_{q}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}\big[\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-2\big]\delta^{p}{}_{q}+e^{\hat{K}}|M|^{2}\big[\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}-1\big]a^{p}a^{\ast}_{q}\

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\Bigg\{\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}}{M^{\dagger}}\Big[\Big(\partial_{i}\hat{K}\xi_{q}+\partial_{i}\xi_{q}\Big)\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}-\xi_{q}\Big]a^{p}+\text{h.c.}\Bigg\}+|M|^{2}a_{r}^{\ast}a^{r}e^{\hat{K}}\delta^{p}{}_{q}

\displaystyle+\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{|M|^{2}}\Bigg(\big<S^{\dagger}_{r}S^{r}\big>a_{q}^{\ast}a^{p}\Big(\hat{\rho}_{1i\ast}\hat{K}^{i\ast}_{i}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}-2\Big)+a_{q}^{\ast}a^{p}\big<S_{r}^{\dagger}S^{r}\big>+\Big\{a_{r}^{\ast}\big<S^{r}\big>a^{p}\big<S_{q}^{\dagger}\big>+{\rm h.c.}\Big\}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\Bigg\{\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{M}\Bigg(a_{r}^{\ast}\big<S^{r}\big>\delta^{p}{}_{q}+a_{q}^{\ast}\big<S^{p}\big>+a_{q}^{\ast}\Big(\hat{\rho}_{1i\ast}\hat{K}^{i\ast}_{i}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}-2\Big)\big<S^{p}\big>\Bigg)+{\rm h.c.}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+e^{\frac{\hat{K}}{2}}\Bigg\{\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}}{M^{\dagger}}a^{r}\xi_{r}+{\rm h.c.}\Bigg\}\delta^{p}{}_{q}\ ,

\displaystyle A_{abc}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}\Bigg\{\Big[\partial_{i}\hat{K}y_{abc}+\partial_{i}y_{abc}\Big]\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}+\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}y_{abc}a^{p}\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle

\displaystyle-\Big[(\Lambda^{-1})^{d}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{i}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}y_{dbc}+(a\leftrightarrow b)+(a\leftrightarrow c)\Bigg\}\ ,

\displaystyle A^{\prime}_{abp}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{M}\Bigg\{-\Big[(\Lambda^{-1})^{c}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{i}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}(Z_{cb}-\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{cb})+(a\leftrightarrow b)\Big]

\displaystyle+\Big[3Z_{ab}-\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{ab}+\rho^{\ast i}\partial_{i}Z_{ab}-\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\rho^{\ast i}\partial^{i^{\ast}}\partial_{i}Z_{ab}\Big]

\displaystyle+\big(\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-3\big)Z_{ab}+\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}(2Z_{ab}-\rho_{1i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{ab})a^{q}\langle S^{\dagger}_{q}\rangle\Bigg\}a^{\ast}_{p}+e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{p}{\cal U}\sigma_{ab}\ ,

\displaystyle A^{\prime\prime}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}\Big[-\sigma_{ab}+\big(\partial_{i}\hat{K}\sigma_{ab}+\partial_{i}\sigma_{ab}\big)\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-\Big((\Lambda^{-1})^{c}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{i}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}\sigma_{cb}+(a\leftrightarrow b)\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle+e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\frac{m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M^{\dagger}}a^{p}<S^{\dagger}_{p}>\sigma_{ab}\ ,

\displaystyle B_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}\Bigg(\Big[\partial_{i}\hat{K}m_{ab}+\partial_{i}m_{ab}\Big]\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-m_{ab}-\Big[(\Lambda^{-1})^{c}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{i}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}m_{cb}+(a\leftrightarrow b)\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}m_{ab}a^{p}\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle+\sigma_{ab}<S^{p}>\partial_{p}{\cal U}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}\Bigg(2Z_{ab}-\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{ab}+\rho^{\ast i}\partial_{i}Z_{ab}-\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\rho^{\ast i}\partial^{i^{\ast}}\partial_{i}Z_{ab}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}(Z_{ab}-\rho_{i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{ab})a^{p}\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle\Bigg)

\displaystyle-|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}\Bigg(\Big[(\Lambda^{-1})^{c}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{i}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}(Z_{cb}-\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{cb})+(a\leftrightarrow b)\Big]-\Big[\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i^{\ast}}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-3\Big]Z_{ab}\Bigg)\

\displaystyle+|M|^{2}a_{q}^{\ast}a^{q}e^{\hat{K}}Z_{ab}+\Bigg\{e^{\frac{\hat{K}}{2}}\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}}{M^{\dagger}}a^{q}\xi_{q}+\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{M}a_{p}^{\ast}\big<S^{p}\big>+{\rm h.c.}\Bigg\}Z_{ab}\ ,

\displaystyle B^{\prime}_{pq}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{M}\Bigg\{a_{p}^{\ast}\big<S_{q}^{\dagger}\big>\big(\hat{\rho}_{1i\ast}\hat{K}^{i\ast}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-1\big)+\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}a^{r}a^{\ast}_{p}\big<S^{\dagger}_{r}S^{\dagger}_{q}\big>\Bigg\}\ ,

\displaystyle C_{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\Big[-2\xi_{p}+\big(\partial_{i}\hat{K}\xi_{p}+\partial_{i}\xi_{p}\big)\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M}e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\Bigg(\Big[\big(\partial^{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}\xi^{\ast q}+\partial^{i^{\ast}}\xi^{\ast q}\big)\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}-\xi^{\ast q}\Big]<S^{\dagger}_{q}>a^{\ast}_{p}\Bigg)+\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}}{M^{\dagger}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\xi_{p}\big<S^{\dagger}_{q}\big>a^{q}

\displaystyle+e^{\hat{K}}\Big[M(M^{\dagger})^{2}\big(\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-2\big)a^{\ast}_{p}+|M|^{2}a^{q}a^{\ast}_{p}<S^{\dagger}_{q}>\Big]+|M|^{2}a_{r}^{\ast}a^{r}e^{\hat{K}}\big<S_{p}^{\dagger}\big>

\displaystyle+\big<S_{p}^{\dagger}\big>\Bigg\{\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}}{M^{\dagger}}a^{r}\xi_{r}+{\rm h.c.}\Bigg\}+\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{|M|^{2}}a^{r}a_{p}^{\ast}\big<S^{\dagger}_{r}\big>+|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}\Big(\hat{\rho}_{i\ast}\hat{K}^{i\ast}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-2\Big)\big<S_{p}^{\dagger}\big>

\displaystyle+\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{M}\Bigg\{a_{q}^{\ast}\big<S^{q}S_{p}^{\dagger}\big>+\big<S_{r}^{\dagger}S^{r}\big>a_{p}^{\ast}\big(\hat{\rho}_{1i\ast}\hat{K}^{i\ast}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-1\big)\Bigg\}+\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{M^{\dagger}}a^{q}\big<S_{q}^{\dagger}S_{p}^{\dagger}\big>\ ,

\displaystyle B^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\Bigg\{\big(\partial_{i}\hat{K}\mu+\partial_{i}\mu\big)\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}+\Big(\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}a^{p}\big<S_{p}^{\dagger}\big>-3\Big)\mu\Bigg\}\ ,

\displaystyle D

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\Bigg\{\big(\partial_{i}\hat{K}\lambda+\partial_{i}\lambda\big)\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}+\Big(\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}a^{p}\big<S_{p}^{\dagger}\big>-3\Big)\lambda\Bigg\}\ .

The hard supersymmetric breaking
terms are

\displaystyle V_{\text{hard}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q_{ap}{}^{a^{\ast}q}\Phi^{\dagger}_{a^{\ast}}\Phi^{a}S^{\dagger}_{q}S^{p}+Q^{\prime}_{pr}{}^{qt}S^{p}S^{r}S^{\dagger}_{q}S^{\dagger}_{t}+\Big(T_{ap}{}^{b^{\ast}}\Phi^{\dagger}_{b^{\ast}}\Phi^{a}S^{p}+\text{h.c}\Big)+\Big(T^{\prime}_{pq}{}^{r}S^{p}S^{q}S^{\dagger}_{r}+\text{h.c}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{1}{6}D_{abc}{}^{p}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}\Phi^{c}S^{\dagger}_{p}+\text{h.c.}\Big)+\Big(\frac{1}{2}D^{\prime}_{abp}{}^{q}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}S^{p}S^{\dagger}_{q}+\text{h.c.}\Big)+\Big(\frac{1}{2}D^{\prime\prime}_{ab}{}^{q}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}{\cal U}S^{\dagger}_{q}+\text{h.c.}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{1}{2}E_{ab}{}^{p}\Phi^{a}\Phi^{b}S^{\dagger}_{p}+\text{h.c.}\Big)+\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2}B^{\prime p}{\cal U}^{2}S_{p}^{\dagger}+\frac{1}{6}D^{\prime p}{\cal U}^{3}S_{p}^{\dagger}+\text{h.c.}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2}e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\frac{m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M^{\dagger}}\big(\lambda{\cal U}^{2}\partial_{p}{\cal U}\big)(S^{p}+<S^{p}>\big)a^{q}S^{\dagger}_{q}+e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\frac{m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M^{\dagger}}\big(\mu{\cal U}\partial_{p}{\cal U}\big)S^{p}a^{q}S^{\dagger}_{q}+\text{h.c.}\Bigg\}

with

\displaystyle Q_{ap}{}^{a^{\ast}q}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{|M|^{2}}\Bigg[\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}(\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i})\ +\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\Big(\partial^{i^{\ast}}\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{b}(\Lambda^{-1})^{b}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{j}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}-\partial_{j}\partial^{i^{\ast}}\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}\Big)\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast j}\Bigg]a^{\ast}_{p}q^{q}\ ,

\displaystyle Q^{\prime}_{pr}{}^{qt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{|M|^{2}}\Big(\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}\Big)\delta^{q}{}_{p}a^{\ast}_{r}a^{t}\ ,

\displaystyle T_{ap}{}^{a^{\ast}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{M}\Bigg[\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}\Big(\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-1+\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}a^{r}\big<S_{r}^{\dagger}\big>\Big)

\displaystyle+\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\Big(\partial^{i^{\ast}}\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{b}(\Lambda^{-1})^{b}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{j}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}-\partial_{j}\partial^{i^{\ast}}\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}\Big)\hat{\rho}^{\ast j}\Bigg]a^{\ast}_{p}\ ,

\displaystyle T^{\prime}_{rq}{}^{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{M}\Big(\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}-1\Big)\delta^{p}{}_{q}a^{\ast}_{r}

\displaystyle+\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{M^{2}}\Big\{a^{t}a^{\ast}_{q}\big<S_{t}^{\dagger}\big>\delta^{p}{}_{r}+2a_{r}^{\ast}a^{p}\big<S_{q}^{\dagger}\big>+a_{q}^{\ast}a^{p}\Big(\hat{\rho}_{1i\ast}\hat{K}^{i\ast}_{i}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}-2\Big)\big<S_{r}^{\dagger}\big>\Big\}\ ,

\displaystyle D_{abc}{}^{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\frac{m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M^{\dagger}}\Bigg\{\Big[\partial_{i}\hat{K}y_{abc}+\partial_{i}y_{abc}\Big]\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}

\displaystyle-\Big[(\Lambda^{-1})^{d}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{i}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}y_{dbc}+(a\leftrightarrow b)+(a\leftrightarrow c)+y_{abc}\Bigg\}a^{p}\ ,

\displaystyle D^{\prime}_{abp}{}^{q}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{|M|^{2}}\Bigg\{-\Big[(\Lambda^{-1})^{c}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{i}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}(Z_{cb}-\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{cb})+(a\leftrightarrow b)\Big]a^{\ast}_{p}+

\displaystyle+\Big[{\color[rgb]{0,0.45,0}2}Z_{ab}-2\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{ab}+\rho_{1}^{\ast i}\partial_{i}Z_{ab}-\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\rho_{1}^{\ast i}\partial^{i^{\ast}}\partial_{i}Z_{ab}\Big]a^{\ast}_{p}+\hat{\rho}_{1i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}Z_{ab}a^{\ast}_{p}\Bigg\}a^{q}\ ,

\displaystyle+e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\frac{m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M^{\dagger}}\sigma_{ab}\partial_{p}{\cal U}a^{q}

\displaystyle D^{\prime\prime}_{ab}{}^{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\frac{m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M^{\dagger}}\Bigg\{\big(\partial_{i}\hat{K}\sigma_{ab}+\partial_{i}\sigma_{ab}\big)\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}-\Big[(\Lambda^{-1})^{c}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{i}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}\sigma_{cd}+(a\leftrightarrow b)\Big]\Bigg\}a^{p}\ ,

\displaystyle E_{ab}{}^{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\{e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\frac{m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M^{\dagger}}\bigg(\Big[\partial_{i}\hat{K}m_{ab}+\partial_{i}m_{ab}\Big]\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}-\Big[(\Lambda^{-1})^{c}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{i}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}m_{cb}+(a\leftrightarrow b)\Big]\bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{M^{\dagger}}\bigg(4Z_{ab}-2\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{ab}+\rho_{1}^{\ast i}\partial_{i}Z_{ab}-\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\rho_{1}^{\ast i}\partial^{i^{\ast}}\partial_{i}Z_{ab}+\big(\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}-3)Z_{ab}

\displaystyle-\Big[(\Lambda^{-1})^{c}{}_{b^{\ast}}\partial_{i}\Lambda^{b^{\ast}}{}_{a}\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}(Z_{cb}-\hat{\rho}_{i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z_{cb})+(a\leftrightarrow b)\Big]\bigg)+\frac{m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M^{\dagger}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\partial_{q}{\cal U}\sigma_{ab}\big<S^{q}\big>

\displaystyle+\frac{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}}{|M|^{2}}a_{q}^{\ast}\big<S^{p}\big>Z_{ab}\Bigg\}a^{p}\ ,

\displaystyle B^{\prime p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}}{M^{\dagger}}e^{\frac{\hat{K}}{2}}\Bigg\{\Big[\partial_{i}\hat{K}\mu+\partial_{i}\mu\Big]\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}-2\mu\Bigg\}a^{p}\ ,

\displaystyle D^{\prime p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}}{M^{\dagger}}e^{\frac{\hat{K}}{2}}\Bigg\{\Big[\partial_{i}\hat{K}\lambda+\partial_{i}\lambda\Big]\hat{\rho}_{1}^{\ast i}-2\lambda\Bigg\}a^{p}\ .

C.3
Application

In this section we are considering a NSW-extension closely related to the NMSSM. We denote here
Q,U,D
the superfields describing the quark/squark sector,
L,E,N
the superfields for the lepton/slepton sector and
H_{U},H_{D}
the two Higgs superfields (see
Table 3.2
). Introduce further two singlet fields
S^{1},S^{2}
and dimensionless fields in the hidden sector
z^{i}
. Denote generically
\Phi^{a}
the fields in the observable sector
and
\tilde{\Phi}^{I}=(\Phi^{a},H_{U},H_{D})
. Recall that

\displaystyle{\cal U}=\mu^{1}S^{2}-\mu^{2}S^{1}\ .

The Kähler potential is taken to be

\displaystyle K(z,z^{\dagger},S,S^{\dagger},\Phi,\Phi^{\dagger})=m_{p}^{2}\hat{K}(z,z^{\dagger})+S^{\dagger}_{p}S^{p}+\sum_{I}{\Lambda_{I}(z,z^{\dagger})}\tilde{\Phi}^{\dagger}_{I}\tilde{\Phi}^{I}+\Big[{Z(z,z^{\dagger})}H_{U}\cdot H_{D}+\text{h.c.}\Big]

and the superpotential

\displaystyle W(z,S,\Phi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{p}\big[\hat{W}_{0}(z)+S^{p}\mu_{p}^{\ast}\hat{W}_{1}(z)\big]+S^{p}\zeta_{p}(z)

\displaystyle-{y_{e}(z)}L\cdot H_{D}E-{y_{d}(z)}Q\cdot H_{D}D+{y_{u}(z)}Q\cdot H_{U}U+{\lambda(z)}{\cal U}H_{D}\cdot H_{U}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{6}{\kappa(z)}{\cal U}^{3}

Several remarks are in order:

1.

We neglect in this first analysis the flavour of particles,
i.e.
, we only consider one family of quarks and leptons;

2.

We do not consider the interactions with neutrinos;

3.

We only consider dimensionless coefficients in the Kähler potential and in the superpotential
\tilde{W}_{k}(z)g^{k}(\mathcal{U},\Phi)
, the dimensionfull terms in
{\cal U},{\cal U}^{2}
or
H_{U}\cdot H_{D}
can be easily taken into consideration if needed. Note that the term
\zeta{\cal U}
is already present in
\tilde{W}_{p}(z)S^{p}=\zeta_{p}S^{p}
.

Before using the results previously established, some care is needed. Indeed, after supersymmetry breaking the kinetic part of the scalar component
\varphi
and
of the fermion component
\chi
of the chiral superfield
\Phi
are not correctly normalised.
Actually the factor
\Lambda\Phi^{\dagger}\Phi
in the Kähler potential leads to

\displaystyle L_{\text{kin scal.}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big<\Lambda\big>\partial_{\mu}\varphi\partial^{\mu}\varphi^{\dagger}\ ,

\displaystyle L_{\text{ kin ferm.}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{i}{2}\big<\Lambda\big>\Big(\chi\sigma^{\mu}\partial_{\mu}\bar{\chi}-\partial_{\mu}\chi\sigma^{\mu}\bar{\chi}\Big)\ .

Thus
\big<\Lambda\big>
must be positive, and in order to have correctly normalised fields, a field redefinition must be performed

\displaystyle\varphi\to\frac{1}{\sqrt{\big<\Lambda\big>}}\varphi\ ,\ \ \chi\to\frac{1}{\sqrt{\big<\Lambda\big>}}\chi\ .

When supergravity is broken in the hidden sector the scalar potential takes the forms

\displaystyle V=V_{\text{SUSY}}+V_{\text{soft}}+V_{\text{hard}}\ .

Recall that the gravitino mass is given by

\displaystyle m_{\frac{3}{2}}=\frac{1}{m_{p}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big<\hat{K}\big>}\big<\hat{W}\big>\ ,

and the scale
M
is defined through

\displaystyle M^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big<\hat{W}_{0}+S^{p}\mu^{\ast}_{p}\hat{W}_{1}\big>\ ,

\displaystyle a_{p}^{\ast}M

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu_{p}^{\ast}\big<\hat{W}_{1}\big>\ .

We also introduce two basic functions

\displaystyle\rho_{{i^{\ast}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{i}\Big(\hat{K}+\ln\frac{\hat{W}}{m_{p}^{2}}\Big)(\hat{K}^{-1})^{i}{}_{i^{\ast}}

\displaystyle\rho_{1,{i^{\ast}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{i}\Big(\hat{K}+\ln\frac{\hat{W}_{1}}{m_{p}}\Big)(\hat{K}^{-1})^{i}{}_{i^{\ast}}

The unbroken supersymmetric part of the potential is

\displaystyle V_{\text{SUSY}}=\partial_{a}W_{m}\partial^{a}\bar{W}_{m}+\partial_{p}W^{\prime}_{m}\partial^{p}\bar{W}^{\prime}_{m}\ ,

where the superpotential is given by

\displaystyle W_{m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle W^{\prime}_{m}+W_{GM}

\displaystyle W^{\prime}_{m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\hat{y}_{e}}L\cdot H_{D}E-{\hat{y}_{d}}Q\cdot H_{D}D+{\hat{y}_{u}}Q\cdot H_{U}U+{\hat{\lambda}}\;{\cal U}H_{D}\cdot H_{U}+\frac{1}{6}{\hat{\kappa}}\;{\cal U}^{3}+{\hat{\zeta}_{p}}\;{S^{p}}

\displaystyle W_{GM}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mu}H_{U}\cdot H_{D}+{y_{p}}S^{p}H_{U}\cdot H_{D}

(C.3)

The terms in
Eq. C.3
come from the Kähler potential, and constitute the usual Giudice-Masiero term supplemented by the corresponding term resulting from
the S-field:

\displaystyle{\mu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{\frac{3}{2}}\Big(\frac{\big<Z\big>}{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{D}}\Lambda_{H_{U}}}}-\frac{\big<\rho_{i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z\big>}{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{D}}\Lambda_{H_{U}}}}\Big)\ ,

\displaystyle{y_{p}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M}\Big(\frac{\big<Z\big>}{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{D}}\Lambda_{H_{U}}}}-\frac{\big<\rho_{1,i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z\big>}{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{D}}\Lambda_{H_{U}}}}\Big)a_{p}^{\ast}\ .

Note that the
y_{p}
term is
M-
suppressed. The Yukawa interaction have two origins : the superpotential and the field redefinition due to the
\Lambda-
term in the Kähler potential:

\displaystyle\hat{y}_{e}=e^{\frac{1}{2}\big<\hat{K}\big>}\frac{\big<y_{e}\big>}{\sqrt{\big<\Lambda_{E}\Lambda_{L}\Lambda_{H_{D}}}\big>}\ ,\hat{y}_{d}=e^{\frac{1}{2}\big<\hat{K}\big>}\frac{\big<y_{d}\big>}{\sqrt{\big<\Lambda_{Q}\Lambda_{D}\Lambda_{H_{D}}}\big>}\ ,\hat{y}_{u}=e^{\frac{1}{2}\big<\hat{K}\big>}\frac{\big<y_{u}\big>}{\sqrt{\big<\Lambda_{Q}\Lambda_{U}\Lambda_{H_{U}}}\big>}\ ,

and

\displaystyle\hat{\kappa}=e^{\frac{1}{2}\big<\hat{K}\big>}\big<\kappa\big>\ ,\ \ \hat{\lambda}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\big<\hat{K}\big>}\frac{\big<\lambda\big>}{\sqrt{\big<\Lambda_{H_{D}}\Lambda_{H_{U}}\big>}}\ ,\ \ \hat{\zeta}_{p}=e^{\frac{1}{2}\big<\hat{K}\big>}{\big<\zeta_{p}\big>}\ .

The soft-supersymmetric breaking part is given by

\displaystyle V_{\text{soft}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\phantom{+}|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}\sum_{I}{\varpi_{I}}\varphi^{\dagger}_{I}\varphi^{I}+\Big(|M|^{2}{\varpi^{\prime}a^{q}a^{\ast}_{p}}+(m^{2})^{q}{}_{p}\Big)S^{p}S^{\dagger}_{q}

\displaystyle+\Bigg[\bigg\{\big|m_{\frac{3}{2}}\big|^{2}\;{b}+\hat{\lambda}m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}\big<{\cal U}\big>\bigg\}\;h_{U}\cdot h_{D}+\text{h.c.}\Bigg]+|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}{\varpi}\Big(S^{p}+\big<S^{p}\big>\Big)\Big(S^{\dagger}_{p}+\big<S^{\dagger}_{p}\big>\Big)

\displaystyle+{\left(|M|^{2}a^{*}_{q}a^{q}e^{\langle\hat{K}\rangle}+\left\{\frac{m^{\dagger}_{3/2}}{M^{\dagger}}a^{q}\hat{\zeta}_{q}+\text{h.c.}\right\}\right)}

\displaystyle{\times\left[(S^{p}+\langle S^{p}\rangle)(S^{\dagger}_{p}+\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle)+\varphi^{\dagger}_{I}\varphi^{I}+\Big\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{U}}\Lambda_{H_{D}}}}\big<Z\big>h_{U}\cdot h_{D}+{\rm h.c.}\Big\}\right]}

\displaystyle+\Bigg\{m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}\bigg(-{A_{e}}\tilde{\ell}\cdot h_{D}\tilde{e}-{A_{d}}\tilde{q}\cdot h_{D}\tilde{d}+{A_{u}}\tilde{q}\cdot h_{U}\tilde{u}+A^{\prime}{\cal U}h_{U}\cdot h_{D}+{A_{p}}\;S^{p}h_{U}\cdot h_{D}

\displaystyle+C_{p}S^{p}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{\kappa}{\cal U}^{2}\big({\cal U}+\big<{\cal U}\big>\big)+\frac{1}{6}{A_{\kappa}}{\cal U}^{3}\bigg)+m_{\frac{3}{2}}C^{\prime}_{p}S^{p}+C^{\prime\prime}_{p}S^{p}+\text{h.c.}\Bigg\}

where we have introduced:

1.

Scalar masses:

\displaystyle{\varpi_{I}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big<\rho_{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\bar{\rho}^{i}-2\Big>+\left<\rho_{i^{\ast}}\Big(\frac{\partial^{i^{\ast}}{\Lambda_{I}}\partial_{i}{\Lambda_{I}}}{\Lambda_{I}^{2}}-\frac{\partial^{i^{\ast}}\partial_{i}{\Lambda_{I}}}{\Lambda_{I}}\Big)\bar{\rho}^{i}\right>\ ,

\displaystyle{\varpi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big<\rho_{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\bar{\rho}^{i}-2\Big>

\displaystyle\varpi^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\big<\hat{K}\big>}\Big<\rho_{1,{i^{\ast}}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\bar{\rho}_{1}^{i}-1\Big>

(C.4)

\displaystyle(m^{2})^{p}{}_{q}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\frac{1}{2}\big<\hat{K}\big>}\Bigg\{\frac{m^{\dagger}_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M^{\dagger}}\Big[(\partial_{i}\hat{K}\zeta_{q}+\partial_{i}\zeta_{q})\bar{\rho}_{1}^{i}-\zeta_{q}\Big]a^{p}+\text{h.c.}\Bigg\}

(C.5)

Note the terms
Eq. C.4
and
Eq. C.5
are controlled by
M
(second line of
V_{\text{soft}}
).

2.

b-
term:

\displaystyle{b}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\big<Z\big>}{{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{D}}\Lambda_{H_{U}}}}}-\frac{\big<\rho_{i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}{Z}\big>}{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{D}}\Lambda_{H_{U}}}}+\frac{\big<\bar{\rho}^{i}\partial_{i}{Z}\big>}{{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{D}}\Lambda_{H_{U}}}}}-\frac{\big<\rho_{i^{\ast}}\bar{\rho}^{i}\partial^{i^{\ast}}\partial_{i}{Z}\big>}{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{D}}\Lambda_{H_{U}}}}

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{\partial_{i}\Lambda_{H_{U}}}{\Lambda_{H_{U}}}+\frac{\partial_{i}\Lambda_{H_{D}}}{\Lambda_{H_{D}}}\Big)\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{U}}\Lambda_{H_{D}}}}\bar{\rho}^{i}(Z-\rho_{i^{\ast}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{U}}\Lambda_{H_{D}}}}\rho_{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\bar{\rho}^{i}Z

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}\Big(\big<Z\big>-\rho_{{i^{\ast}}}\big<\partial^{{i^{\ast}}}Z\big>\Big)a^{p}\big<S^{\dagger}_{p}\big>\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Lambda_{H_{U}}\Lambda_{H_{D}}}}

This term is one of the signature of the Giudice-Masiero mechanism (note that
b=0
if
Z=0
). It is supplement by a
M-
suppressed term coming from the vev of S.

3.

trilinear
HHS-
term

\displaystyle{A_{p}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{{\big<\Lambda_{H_{U}}\Lambda_{H_{D}}\big>}}}\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}}{M}\Bigg[-\bigg(\frac{\big<\partial_{i}\Lambda_{H_{U}}\big>}{\Lambda_{H_{U}}}+\frac{\big<\partial_{i}\Lambda_{H_{D}}\big>}{\Lambda_{H_{D}}}\bigg)(Z-\rho_{1{i^{\ast}}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z)\big<\bar{\rho}^{i}\big>

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-Z-\rho_{1{i^{\ast}}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z+\bar{\rho}^{i}\partial_{i}Z-\rho_{1{i^{\ast}}}\bar{\rho}^{i}\partial_{i}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z+(\rho_{1{i^{\ast}}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\bar{\rho}^{i})Z

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}(Z-\rho_{1{i^{\ast}}}\partial^{i^{\ast}}Z)a^{q}\big<S^{\dagger}_{q}\big>\Bigg]a^{\ast}_{p}

and the trilinear term
{\cal U}HH

\displaystyle A^{\prime}=\Bigg(\big(\partial_{i}\hat{K}\hat{\lambda}+\partial_{i}\hat{\lambda}\big)\bar{\rho}^{i}-\bigg(\frac{\big<\partial_{i}\Lambda_{H_{U}}\big>}{\Lambda_{H_{U}}}+\frac{\big<\partial_{i}\Lambda_{H_{D}}\big>}{\Lambda_{H_{D}}}\bigg)\bar{\rho}^{i}\hat{\lambda}+{\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}a^{p}\big<S^{\dagger}_{p}\big>}\hat{\lambda}\Bigg).

4.

Trilinear terms:

\displaystyle{A_{I}}=\Bigg[\big<\partial_{i}\hat{K}\big>\hat{y}_{I}+\partial_{i}{\hat{y}_{I}}-\frac{\big<\partial_{i}\Lambda_{I}\big>}{\big<\Lambda_{I}\big>}{\hat{y}_{I}}-\frac{\big<\partial_{i}\Lambda_{I^{\prime}}\big>}{\big<\Lambda_{I^{\prime}}\big>}{\hat{y}_{I}}-\frac{\big<\partial_{i}\Lambda_{H_{I}}\Big>}{\big<\Lambda_{H_{I}}\big>}{\hat{y}_{I}}\Bigg]\big<\bar{\rho}^{i}\big>+\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}a^{p}\big<S^{\dagger}_{p}\big>\hat{y}_{I}

H_{I}
being the Higgs that couples to the scalar
\Phi^{I}
as
y_{I}\Phi^{I}\cdot H_{H_{I}}\Phi^{I^{\prime}}
. The notation
\partial_{i}\hat{y}_{I}
is a shorthand notation for
\big<e^{\frac{1}{2}\hat{K}}\partial_{i}y/\sqrt{\Lambda_{I}\Lambda_{I^{\prime}}\Lambda_{H_{I}}}\big>
.

5.

The linear term is given by

\displaystyle C_{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\hat{\zeta}_{p}+\big(\partial_{i}\hat{K}\hat{\zeta}_{p}+\partial_{i}\hat{\zeta}_{p}\big)\bar{\rho}^{i}+\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}\hat{\zeta}_{p}a^{q}\big<S^{\dagger}_{q}\big>

\displaystyle C_{p}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\frac{1}{M}}\Big(\big[(\partial^{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}{\hat{\bar{\zeta}}}^{q}+\partial^{i^{\ast}}{\hat{\bar{\zeta}}}^{q})\rho_{1{i^{\ast}}}-{\hat{\bar{\zeta}}}^{q}\big]{\big<S^{\dagger}_{q}\big>\big.a^{\ast}_{p}}\Big)

\displaystyle C^{\prime\prime}_{p}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\hat{K}}\Bigg[M(M^{\dagger})^{2}\big(\rho_{i^{\ast}}\hat{K}^{i^{\ast}}{}_{i}\bar{\rho}^{i}-2)+|M|^{2}a^{q}\big<S^{\dagger}_{q}\big>\Bigg]a^{\ast}_{p}\ .

6.

{\cal U}^{3}
term :

\displaystyle A_{\kappa}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big(\partial_{i}\hat{K}\hat{\kappa}+\partial_{i}\hat{\kappa}\big)\hat{\rho}^{\ast i}+\Big(\frac{1}{M^{\dagger}}a^{p}\big<S_{p}^{\dagger}\big>-3\Big)\hat{\kappa}

The hard supersymmetric breaking terms are given by

\displaystyle V_{\text{hard}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}\Bigg[\sum_{I}Q_{Ip}{}^{q}\varphi^{\dagger}_{I}\varphi^{I}S^{\dagger}_{q}S^{p}+Q^{\prime}_{r}{}^{t}S^{\dagger}_{p}S^{p}S^{\dagger}_{t}S^{r}+\Big\{\sum_{I}T_{Ip}{}\varphi^{\dagger}_{I}\varphi^{I}S^{p}+\text{h.c.}\Big\}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\Big\{T^{\prime}_{r}S^{\dagger}_{p}S^{p}S^{r}+\text{h.c.}\Big\}\Bigg]+\Bigg[\Big\{|m_{\frac{3}{2}}|^{2}E^{q}{}+\frac{m_{\frac{3}{2}}^{\dagger}}{M^{\dagger}}\hat{\lambda}\big<{\cal U}\big>a^{q}\Big\}h_{U}\cdot h_{D}S^{\dagger}_{q}+\text{h.c.}\Bigg]

Appendix D
Vanishing of the cosmological constant, minimisation equations and mass matrice of the S2MSSM

In this appendix we give the general form of the mass matrix for the general potential. We recall that the superpotential and the Kähler potential take the form

\displaystyle W(z,S,\widetilde{\Phi})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{p\ell}\left(W_{1,0}(z)+S^{p}W_{1,p}(z)\right)+\ W_{0}(z,\widetilde{\Phi},\mathcal{U}^{12})+S^{p}W_{0,p}(z)\ ,

\displaystyle K

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{p}^{2}zz^{\dagger}+S_{p}^{\dagger}S^{p}+\Phi_{a*}^{\dagger}\Lambda^{a*}{}_{a}(z,z^{\dagger})\Phi^{a}\ .

with only one field
z
from the hidden sector. We use the definitions introduced in Section
4.2
. The potential at the minimum is easily obtained from
Eq. 4.22
:

\displaystyle\big\langle V\big\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{p}^{2}\big(\big|m^{\prime}_{3/2}\big|^{2}-3\big|m_{3/2}\big|^{2}\big)+e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big(\sum\limits_{p}\big|{\cal I}_{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{p}\omega_{0}\rangle\big|^{2}+M_{4}^{4}\langle\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\partial^{a^{\ast}}\bar{\omega}_{0}(\Lambda^{-1})^{a}{}_{a^{\ast}}\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle+\big|m_{3/2}\big|^{2}M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{a}\rangle\langle\phi^{\dagger}_{a^{\ast}}\rangle{\cal S}^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}+\big|m_{3/2}\big|^{2}\langle S^{p}\rangle\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle T

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}_{a^{\ast}}\rangle\langle\phi^{a}\rangle\langle\Lambda^{a^{\ast}}{}_{a}\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle\langle S^{p}\rangle\Big)\Bigg\{\Big(\bar{m}_{3/2}\langle S^{q}\rangle{\cal I}_{q}+\mbox{h.c.}\Big)

\displaystyle+e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big(\sum\limits_{r}\big|{\cal I}_{r}\big|^{2}+M_{4}^{3}\bar{\cal I}^{r}\langle\partial_{r}\omega_{0}\rangle+M_{4}^{3}{{\cal I}}_{r}\langle\partial^{r}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle\Big)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[M_{4}^{4}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\partial^{p}\partial^{q}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle+M_{4}^{2}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\partial^{p}\partial^{b^{*}}\bar{\omega}_{0}(\Lambda^{-1})^{b}{}_{b^{*}}\rangle\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}^{2}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Bigg\{(\mathfrak{d}^{z}\bar{\cal I}^{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\mathfrak{d}^{z}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)\langle\partial_{a}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle-(\bar{\cal I}^{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)\langle\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 51.21504pt-(\mathfrak{d}^{z}\bar{\cal I}^{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\mathfrak{d}^{z}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)\langle\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\rangle\langle(\Lambda^{-1})^{b}{}_{a^{*}}\partial_{z}\Lambda^{a^{*}}{}_{a}\rangle+(\bar{\cal I}^{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)\langle\phi^{b}\partial_{b}\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 51.21504pt-(\mathfrak{d}^{z}\bar{\cal I}^{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\mathfrak{d}^{z}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)\langle\phi^{c}\partial_{b}\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\rangle(\Lambda^{-1})^{b}{}_{a^{*}}\partial_{z}\Lambda^{a^{*}}{}_{c}\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 51.21504pt-M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{q}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\phi^{\dagger}_{b^{*}}\partial^{a^{*}}\partial^{p}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle(\Lambda^{-1})^{b}{}_{a^{*}}\partial_{z}\Lambda^{a^{*}}{}_{b}\rangle+M_{4}^{3}\langle\phi^{\dagger}_{a^{*}}\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\partial^{a^{*}}\partial^{p}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\langle\phi^{\dagger}_{a^{*}}\rangle\Big[-m_{3/2}(\bar{\cal I}^{p}+2M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)\langle\Lambda^{a^{*}}{}_{a}\rangle+m^{\prime}_{3/2}(\mathfrak{d}^{z}\bar{\cal I}^{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\mathfrak{d}^{z}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)\tilde{\cal S}^{a^{*}}{}_{a}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\langle(\phi^{\dagger}\Lambda_{a}\rangle\Big[M_{4}^{3}{\cal I}_{q}\langle\partial^{p}\partial^{q}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle+\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}(\bar{\cal I}^{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)\langle S^{q}\rangle{\cal I}_{q}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}^{2}}{m_{p}^{4}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Bigg\{-\Big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}\Lambda\phi\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}S\rangle\Big)\Big(\bar{\cal I}^{p}+2M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle\Big)\langle\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 65.44142pt+\Big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}\tilde{S}\phi\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}S\rangle\Big)\Big(\mathfrak{d}^{z}\bar{\cal I}^{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\mathfrak{d}^{z}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle\Big)\langle\partial_{a}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle\Bigg\}

\bullet:\phi-\phi
sector

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M_{4}^{2}}\big<\partial_{a}\partial_{b}V\big>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[M_{4}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\partial_{p}\omega_{0}\rangle(\bar{\cal I}^{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)+M_{4}^{2}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\partial_{c}\omega_{0}\partial^{c^{*}}\bar{\omega}_{0}(\Lambda^{-1})^{c}{}_{c^{*}}\rangle\Big]

\displaystyle+e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}M_{4}\Big[\bar{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle+\bar{m}_{3/2}(-3\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\rangle+R^{c}{}_{a}\langle\partial_{b}\partial_{c}\omega_{0}\rangle+R^{c}{}_{b}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{c}\omega_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 59.75095pt+R^{d}{}_{c}\langle\phi^{c}\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\partial_{d}\omega_{0}\rangle+\langle S^{p}\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\partial_{p}\omega_{0}\rangle)\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}^{3}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\langle\phi^{\dagger}_{b^{*}}\rangle\Big[-3\bar{m}_{3/2}(\langle\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\Lambda^{b^{*}}{}_{b}\rangle+\langle\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\Lambda^{b^{*}}{}_{a}\rangle)

\displaystyle\hskip 91.04872pt+\bar{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}(\langle\partial_{a}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle\tilde{\cal S}^{b^{*}}{}_{b}+\langle\partial_{b}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle\tilde{\cal S}^{b^{*}}{}_{a})\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[-2\bar{m}_{3/2}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\rangle\big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}\Lambda\phi\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}S\rangle\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 65.44142pt+\bar{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle\big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}\tilde{\cal S}\phi\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}S\rangle\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\frac{M_{4}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle\bar{\cal I}^{q}+\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\rangle\langle S^{\dagger}_{q}\rangle\bar{\cal I}^{q}\Big]\Big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}\Lambda\phi\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}S\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}^{3}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[\langle\phi^{\dagger}_{a^{*}}\Lambda^{a^{*}}{}_{a}\rangle\big({\cal I}^{p}\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\rangle+\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}\langle\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\rangle\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle\bar{\cal I}^{p}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\langle\phi^{\dagger}_{a^{*}}\Lambda^{a^{*}}{}_{b}\rangle\big({\cal I}^{P}\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\rangle+\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}\langle\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\rangle\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle\bar{\cal I}^{p}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\rangle\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle(\bar{\cal I}^{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{p}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)

\displaystyle\hskip 36.98866pt+M_{4}^{3}\big(-\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}(\Lambda^{-1})^{c}{}_{a^{*}}\partial^{z}\Lambda^{b^{*}}_{c}\phi^{\dagger}_{b^{*}}\partial^{a^{*}}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle+\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\phi^{\dagger}_{a^{*}}\partial^{a^{*}}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle\big)\Big]

\bullet:S-S
sector

\displaystyle\big<\partial_{p}\partial_{q}V\big>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\partial_{r}\omega_{0}\rangle(\bar{\cal I}^{r}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{r}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)+M_{4}^{4}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\partial^{a^{*}}\bar{\omega}_{0}(\Lambda^{-1})^{a}{}_{a^{*}}\rangle\Big]

\displaystyle+M_{4}^{3}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\big[\bar{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle+\bar{m}_{3/2}\big\{-\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle+R^{b}{}_{a}\langle\phi^{a}\partial_{b}\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle+\langle S^{r}\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\partial_{r}\omega_{0}\rangle\big\}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\langle S^{\dagger}_{q}\rangle\Big[-\bar{m}_{3/2}({\cal I}_{p}+2M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{p}\omega_{0}\rangle)+\bar{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}(\mathfrak{d}_{z}{\cal I}_{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{p}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle)\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle\Big[-\bar{m}_{3/2}({\cal I}_{q}+2M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle)+\bar{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}(\mathfrak{d}_{z}{\cal I}_{q}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{q}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle)\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Bigg\{\langle S^{\dagger}_{q}\rangle\bar{\cal I}^{r}\Big[M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{r}\partial_{p}\omega_{0}\rangle+\frac{1}{m^{2}_{p}}\langle S^{\dagger}_{r}\rangle({\cal I}_{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{p}\omega_{0}\rangle)\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 51.21504pt+\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle\bar{\cal I}^{r}\Big[M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{r}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle+\frac{1}{m^{2}_{p}}\langle S^{\dagger}_{r}\rangle({\cal I}_{q}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle)\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}^{3}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[\langle S^{\dagger}_{r}\rangle\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle(\bar{\cal I}^{r}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{r}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)

\displaystyle\hskip 51.21504pt+M_{4}^{3}\langle\phi^{\dagger}_{a^{*}}\rangle\Big(\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\partial^{a^{\ast}}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle-\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\partial^{b^{*}}\bar{\omega}_{0}(\Lambda^{-1})^{b}{}_{b^{*}}\partial^{z}\Lambda^{a^{*}}{}_{b}\rangle\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}^{3}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}\Lambda\phi\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}S\rangle\Big)\bar{\cal I}^{r}\Big(\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\partial_{r}\omega_{0}\rangle+\frac{1}{m^{2}_{p}}\langle S^{\dagger}_{r}\rangle\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}^{3}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[-2\bar{m}_{3/2}\Big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}\Lambda\phi\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}S\rangle\Big)\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 65.44142pt+\bar{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}\Big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}\tilde{\cal S}\phi\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}S\rangle\Big)\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle\Big]

\bullet:S-\phi
sector

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M_{4}}\big<\partial_{a}\partial_{p}V\big>

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[M_{4}^{2}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle(\bar{\cal I}^{q}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{q}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\partial_{p}\omega_{0}\partial^{a^{*}}\bar{\omega}_{0}(\Lambda^{-1})^{b}{}_{b^{*}}\rangle\Big]

\displaystyle+M^{2}_{4}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[\bar{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{p}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle+\bar{m}_{3/2}\big\{-2\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{p}\omega_{0}\rangle+R^{b}{}_{a}\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\rangle+R^{b}{}_{c}\langle\phi^{c}\partial_{p}\partial_{a}\partial_{b}\omega_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\langle S^{q}\partial_{a}\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle\big\}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Bigg\{M_{4}\langle\phi^{\dagger}_{a^{*}}\rangle\Big[-\bar{m}_{3/2}({\cal I}_{p}+2\langle\partial_{p}\omega_{0}\rangle)\langle\Lambda^{a^{*}}{}_{a}\rangle+\bar{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}(\mathfrak{d}_{z}{\cal I}_{p}+\langle\partial_{p}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle)\tilde{\cal S}^{a^{*}}{}_{a}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 59.75095pt+M_{4}^{2}\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle\Big[-2\bar{m}_{3/2}\langle\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\rangle+\bar{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}\langle\partial_{a}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Bigg\{M_{4}\langle\phi^{\dagger}_{a^{*}}\Lambda^{a^{*}}{}_{a}\rangle\bar{\cal I}^{q}\Big[\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}({\cal I}_{p}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{p}\omega_{0}\rangle)\langle S^{\dagger}_{q}\rangle+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 54.06006pt+M_{4}^{4}\langle S^{\dagger}_{p}\rangle\bar{\cal I}^{q}\Big[\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}\langle S^{\dagger}_{q}\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\rangle+\langle\partial_{q}\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\rangle\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}^{2}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big[\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{p}\omega_{0}S^{\dagger}_{q}\rangle(\bar{\cal I}^{q}+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial^{q}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle)+M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{p}\phi^{\dagger}_{a^{*}}\partial^{a^{*}}\bar{\omega}_{0}\rangle\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 54.06006pt-M_{4}^{3}\langle\partial_{p}\partial_{a}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\phi^{\dagger}_{b^{*}}\partial^{a^{*}}\bar{\omega}_{0}(\Lambda^{-1})^{b}{}_{a^{*}}\partial^{z}\Lambda^{b^{*}}{}_{b}\rangle\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}^{2}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}\Lambda\phi\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}S\rangle\Big)\bar{\cal I}^{q}\Big(\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}\langle S^{\dagger}_{q}\partial_{p}\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\rangle+\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{p}\partial_{q}\omega_{0}\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{M_{4}^{2}}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\frac{1}{2}\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Bigg\{-2\bar{m}_{3/2}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{p}\omega_{0}\rangle\Big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}\Lambda\phi\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}S\rangle\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 65.44142pt+\bar{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}\langle\partial_{a}\partial_{p}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle\Big(M_{4}^{2}\langle\phi^{\dagger}\tilde{\cal S}\phi\rangle+\langle S^{\dagger}S\rangle\Big)\Bigg\}\ .

Note that the formulas presented in the Subsection
4.4
can be directly obtained from those general results by taking :

\displaystyle\omega_{0}(\Phi,S,z)=\omega_{0}(\Phi,z)\quad,\quad\Lambda^{a}{}_{a^{\ast}}(z,z^{{\dagger}})=\delta^{a}_{a^{\ast}}\quad,\quad\langle\phi^{a}\rangle=\langle S^{p}\rangle=0\;.

Appendix E
Screening property of the mass matrix

We show in this subsection an interesting decoupling property of the mass matrix in the basis
\{z,S^{p},\Phi^{a}\}
. Let
M_{1}\in{\cal M}_{n_{1}}(\mathbb{R}),M_{2}\in{\cal M}_{n_{2}}(\mathbb{R})
where
M_{1}
and
M_{2}
are symmetric matrices and let
P\in{\cal M}_{n_{2},n_{1}}(\mathbb{R})
.
Set
n=n_{1}+n_{2}
and consider a symmetric
n\times n
matrix of the form

\displaystyle M=\begin{pmatrix}\phantom{\varepsilon}M_{1}&\varepsilon P^{t}\\
\varepsilon P&\varepsilon M_{2}\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}M_{1}&\sqrt{\eta}P^{t}\\
\sqrt{\eta}P&\varepsilon M_{2}\end{pmatrix}

(E.1)

with
\varepsilon\sim 0
.

Proposition E.0.1

Let
\overline{\lambda}_{n}
, be the eigenvalues of the matrices
M_{1}
and
\varepsilon M_{2}
defined as

\displaystyle\overline{\lambda}_{n}=\begin{cases}\tilde{\lambda}_{n},\ n=1,\ \cdots,\ n_{1}\ \mathrm{with}\ \mathrm{det}(\tilde{\lambda}_{n}\mathds{1}-M_{1})=0\\
\varepsilon\tilde{\lambda}_{n},\ n=1+n_{1},\ \cdots,\ n_{1}+n_{2}\ \mathrm{with}\ \mathrm{det}(\tilde{\lambda}_{n}\mathds{1}-M_{2})=0\end{cases}

(E.2)

We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\overline{\lambda}_{n}\mathds{1}-M)=0+\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{2})\ .

Proof.

Taking a general matrix
M
defined by

\displaystyle M=\begin{pmatrix}A&B\\
C&D\end{pmatrix}

(E.3)

with
A,\ B,\ C
and
D
some matrices, the determinant of
M
can be obtained using

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(M)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\mathrm{det}A)\mathrm{det}(D-CA^{-1}B)\quad\mathrm{if\ }A\mathrm{\ is\ invertible}\ ,

(E.4)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\mathrm{det}D)\mathrm{det}(A-BD^{-1}C)\quad\mathrm{if\ }D\mathrm{\ is\ invertible}\ .

(E.5)

In our case, the relation
Eq. E.4
become:

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-M)=\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1})\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2}-\eta P(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1})^{-1}P^{t})\ .

(E.6)

with
\eta=\varepsilon^{2}
. Writing for simplicity

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} A&=-\frac{1}{\lambda}M_{2}\ ,\\
B&=-\frac{1}{\lambda}P\left(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1}\right)^{-1}P^{t}\ ,\end{aligned}

the relation
Eq. E.6
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1})\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2}-\eta P(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1})^{-1}P^{t})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda^{n_{2}}\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1})\mathrm{det}(\mathds{1}+\epsilon A+\eta B)\ .

Using the identity

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\mathds{1}+\epsilon A+\eta B)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{n}\frac{\eta^{k}}{k!}\frac{d^{k}}{d\eta^{k}}\mathrm{det}(\mathds{1}+\epsilon A+\eta B)\Big|_{\eta=0}

(E.7)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\mathds{1}+\varepsilon A)\Bigg(1+\eta\mathrm{Tr}\left(X\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\eta^{2}\Big(\mathrm{Tr}\left(X\right)^{2}-\mathrm{Tr}\left(X^{2}\right)\Big)+\cdots\Bigg)

with
X=\frac{B}{\mathds{1}+\varepsilon A}
, we have easily

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-M)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1})\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2})+\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{2})\ .

Taking the eigenvalues
\overline{\lambda}_{n}
of
M_{1}
and
\varepsilon M_{2}
(see
Eq. E.2
), we then get:

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\overline{\lambda}_{n}\mathds{1}-M)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\overline{\lambda}_{n}\mathds{1}-M_{1})\mathrm{det}(\overline{\lambda}_{n}\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2})+\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{2})=0+\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{2})

which completes the proof.

\blacksquare

Proposition E.0.2

Let
\lambda_{n}
be the eigenvalues of
M
:

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\lambda_{n}\mathds{1}-M)=0\ .

(E.8)

The eigenvalues of the matrix
M
are the eigenvalues of the matrix
M_{1}
and
\varepsilon M_{2}
up to
\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{2})
contribution,

\displaystyle\lambda_{n}=\overline{\lambda}_{n}+\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{2}),\ n=1,\ \cdots,\ n_{1}+n_{2}\ .

Proof.

In the limit
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
, the eigenvalues
\lambda_{n}
of the matrix
Eq. E.1
are

\displaystyle\lambda_{n}(\varepsilon)=\begin{cases}\tilde{\lambda}_{n},\ n=1,\ \cdots,\ n_{1}\\
0,\ n=1+n_{1},\ \cdots,\ n_{1}+n_{2}\end{cases}

Writing the eigenvalues as a power series in
\varepsilon
:

\displaystyle\lambda_{n}(\varepsilon)=\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}a_{n_{i}}\varepsilon^{i}\ ,

(E.9)

we can separate them in two different types:
a_{n_{0}}\neq 0
for
n=1,\ \cdots,\ n_{1}
(defined as
type-I
) and
a_{n_{0}}=0
for
n=1+n_{1}\ \cdots,\ n_{1}+n_{2}
(defined as
type-II
). Using the relations
Eq. E.4
and
Eq. E.5
, we have

(i)

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-M)=0\Leftrightarrow\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2}-\eta P(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1})^{-1}P^{t})=0

(E.10)

if
\lambda
is not a
type-I
,

(ii)

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-M)=0\Leftrightarrow\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1}-\eta P^{t}(\lambda\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2})^{-1}P)=0

(E.11)

if
\lambda
is not a
type-II
.

We first consider the case (i). By analysing the form of the determinant
Eq. E.10
, we see that it is equivalent to a polynomial in
\lambda
,
\varepsilon
and
\eta

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2}-\eta P(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1})^{-1}P^{t})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}\geq n_{2}}c_{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3}}\lambda^{\alpha_{1}}\varepsilon^{\alpha_{2}}\eta^{\alpha_{3}}

In the case where
\lambda
are the exact eigenvalues
Eq. E.9
, then we can write

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2}-\eta P(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1})^{-1}P^{t})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}\geq n_{2}}c_{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3}}\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\cdots i_{\alpha_{1}}}a_{i_{1}}a_{i_{2}}\cdots a_{i_{\alpha_{1}}}

(E.12)

\displaystyle\times\varepsilon^{\alpha_{2}+2\alpha_{3}+\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}i_{k}}\ .

Note that the relation
E.10
is valid at all order of
\varepsilon
,
i.e.
, all the coefficients of the polynomial
E.12
must be equal to zero. Following Equation
E.10
in the limit
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
, we see that
a_{0}=0
and so
\lambda(\varepsilon)=\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}a_{i}\varepsilon^{i}
. The index
i_{k}
in
Eq. E.12
are then all different from zero and so

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}i_{k}\geq\alpha_{1}\ .

Using the relation
\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}\geq n_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}i_{k}+\alpha_{2}+2\alpha_{3}\geq n_{2}+\alpha_{3}\ .

Since
\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}\geq n_{2}
, the lowest order in
\varepsilon
is
n_{2}
which is only obtained for
\alpha_{3}=0
and
i_{1}=i_{2}=\cdots=1
,
i.e.
, the term in
\varepsilon^{n_{2}}
contains only
a_{1}
and no dependency in
\eta
. Using
Eq. E.7
and
Eq. E.10
, we deduce that the term in
\varepsilon^{n_{2}}
is
\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2})
. Since the relation
E.10
holds order by order in
\varepsilon
, we then get

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(a_{1}\varepsilon\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2})=\varepsilon^{n_{2}}\mathrm{det}(a_{1}\mathds{1}-M_{2})=0\ .

(E.13)

In the first order of
\varepsilon
,
\lambda
gives the eigenvalues of
\varepsilon M_{2}
.

We must also check that the higher order in
\varepsilon^{\ell}
(
\ell>n_{2}
) are not only function of
a_{1}
in such a way that possibles constraints that appear in higher order are still consistent with the result
Eq. E.13
. It is indeed the case. We define
\ell=\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}i_{k}+\alpha_{2}+2\alpha_{3}
with
\alpha_{3}\geq 1
,
i_{k}=1\ \forall k\in\{1,\ \cdots,\alpha_{1}\}
and with
\alpha_{1},\ \alpha_{2},\ \alpha_{3}
satisfying
\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}\geq n_{2}
. We assume that the same order is obtained for
\alpha_{1}
fixed and
\alpha_{3}-r
,
\alpha_{2}+r
. We then obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}i^{\prime}_{k}+\alpha_{2}+2\alpha_{3}-r=\ell=\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}i_{k}+\alpha_{2}+2\alpha_{3}\Rightarrow\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}i^{\prime}_{k}=\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}i_{k}+r,\ \mathrm{i.e.},\exists k,\ \ i^{\prime}_{k}\geq 2\ .

The factor
a_{1}
is then not the only one that appears for higher orders terms, which remain the result
Eq. E.13
true.

The method is the same for the case (ii). We introduce artificially a parameter
r
such that:

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1}-\eta P^{t}(\lambda\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2})^{-1}P)\rightarrow\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-rM_{1}-\eta P^{t}(\lambda\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2})^{-1}P)\ .

From the Equation
E.11
, we see in the limit
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
that
a_{0}\neq 0
. The determinant can also be written in a polynomial form

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-rM_{1}-\eta P^{t}(\lambda\mathds{1}-\varepsilon M_{2})^{-1}P)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}\geq n_{1}}c_{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3}}\lambda^{\alpha_{1}}r^{\alpha_{2}}\eta^{\alpha_{3}}

(E.14)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}\geq n_{2}}c_{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{3}}\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}\cdots i_{\alpha_{1}}=0}^{\infty}a_{i_{1}}a_{i_{2}}\cdots a_{i_{\alpha_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times\varepsilon^{2\alpha_{3}+\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}i_{k}}

with

\displaystyle\left.\begin{aligned} \displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}i_{k}=0\\
a_{i_{k}}=1\end{aligned}\right\}\ \mathrm{for}\ \alpha_{1}=0\ .

The lowest order in
\varepsilon
is zero for
\alpha_{3}=0
and
i_{k}=0\,\forall k=1,\ \cdots,\ \alpha_{1}
with
\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=n_{1}
. It is then independent of
\eta
. Cancel the lowest order of
\varepsilon
in
Eq. E.11
is then equivalent to

\displaystyle\mathrm{det}(a_{0}\mathds{1}-M_{1})=0

(E.15)

Moreover, since the power of
\varepsilon
in
Eq. E.14
has the form
\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}i_{k}+2\alpha_{3}
, the order 1 in
\varepsilon
is also given by
\alpha_{3}=0
and
\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\alpha_{1}}=1
(so
i_{k}=0\ \forall k
except for one
k^{\ast}
giving
i_{k^{\ast}}=1
). The order 1 in
\varepsilon
is then given by

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=n_{1}}c_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}a_{1}a_{0}^{\alpha_{1}-1}\varepsilon

(E.16)

and included in
\mathrm{det}(\lambda\mathds{1}-M_{1})
. Since the relation
E.11
is valid for all the order in
\varepsilon
, the order one in
\varepsilon

Eq. E.16
must vanish. We have then:

\displaystyle a_{1}=0\ ,\quad\text{or}\quad\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=n_{1}}c_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}a_{0}^{\alpha_{1}-1}=0\ .

(E.17)

However, since the relation
Eq. F.1
is polynomial in order
n_{1}
in
a_{0}
and the second equation in
Eq. E.17
corresponds to a polynomial of order
n_{1}-1
in
a_{0}
, they are then incompatible, leading then to
a_{1}=0
.

\blacksquare

Appendix F
Eigenvalues and eigenvectors of the mass matrix in the
\{S^{p},z\}
basis

This appendix present the analysis of the structure of the mass matrix
\mathcal{M}
in the hybrid/hidden subsector mentionned in
4.3.3
and the computation of their corresponding eigenvectors.

Due to the structure of the mass matrix
\mathcal{M}
in the basis
\{S^{p},z\}
it is easy to obtain all eigenvalues and eigenvectors. We recall that the element of the mass matrix take the form

\displaystyle{\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}V}{\partial S^{p}\partial S_{q}^{\dagger}}\right\rangle}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{p}{}^{q}|\xi_{3/2}|^{2}|m^{\prime}_{3/2}|^{2}+\frac{e^{|\left\langle z\right\rangle|^{2}}}{m_{p}^{2}}\left(4|\xi_{3/2}|^{2}-1\right)\mathcal{I}_{p}\overline{\mathcal{I}}^{q}\ ,

\displaystyle m_{p}^{-1}{\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}V}{\partial S^{p}\partial z^{\dagger}}\right\rangle}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{e^{|\left\langle z\right\rangle|^{2}/2}}{m_{p}}\left(\overline{m}^{\prime}_{3/2}-2\overline{\xi}_{3/2}\overline{m}^{\prime\prime}_{3/2}\right)\mathcal{I}_{p}\ ,

\displaystyle m_{p}^{-2}{\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}V}{\partial z\partial z^{\dagger}}\right\rangle}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{m_{p}^{2}}e^{\big|\langle z\rangle\big|^{2}}\Bigg[\big|{\cal I}\big|^{2}\Big(1+4|\xi_{3/2}|^{2}\Big)+M_{4}^{4}\sum\limits_{a}\Big(\Big|\langle\partial_{a}\mathfrak{d}_{z}\omega_{0}\rangle\Big|^{2}+\Big|\langle\partial_{a}\omega_{0}\rangle\Big|^{2}\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\big|m^{\prime\prime}_{3/2}\big|^{2}-2\big|m_{3/2}\big|^{2}\ .

We first rewrite the mass matrix in a more general form by highlighting the
{\cal I}_{p}
dependencies:

\displaystyle{\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}V}{\partial S^{p}\partial S_{p}^{\dagger}}\right\rangle}=a+b|\mathcal{I}_{p}|^{2},\quad{\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}V}{\partial S^{p}\partial S_{q}^{\dagger}}\right\rangle}=b\mathcal{I}_{p}\overline{\mathcal{I}}^{q}\ ,

\displaystyle m_{p}^{-1}{\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}V}{\partial S^{p}\partial z^{\dagger}}\right\rangle}=c\mathcal{I}_{p}\ ,\quad m_{p}^{-2}{\left\langle\frac{\partial^{2}V}{\partial z\partial z^{\dagger}}\right\rangle}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle d\ .

The
(n+1)\times(n+1)
squared mass matrix can then be written in the following form:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{2}\equiv a\mathbb{I}+\mathbb{A}\ ,\quad\text{with}\quad\mathbb{A}=\begin{pmatrix}[B]&[C]\\
[C^{\dagger}]&[D]\end{pmatrix},

where
\mathbb{A}
is a block matrix and we introduce
[B],[C],[D]
,
n\times n
,
1\times n
and
1\times 1
matrices respectively:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \left[B\right]_{p}{}^{q}&=b\mathcal{I}_{p}\overline{\mathcal{I}}^{q}\ ,\\
\left[C\right]_{p}&=c\mathcal{I}_{p},\ [C^{\dagger}]^{q}=c^{*}\overline{\mathcal{I}}^{q}\left[D\right]_{1}\!\!{}^{1}&=d-a\ .\end{aligned}

The matrix
\mathbb{A}
and
a\mathbb{I}
commute so are simultaneously diagonalisable.
Furthermore, looking to the
n\times n
submatrix
\mathbb{B}
of the matrix
\mathbb{A}
in the
S^{p}-
space we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{B}=bI\otimes I^{\dagger}

where
I
is a vector and
I^{\dagger}
a covector of
\mathbb{C}^{n}
. Thus the kernel of
\mathbb{B}
is
(n-1)
-dimensional since the image of
I^{\dagger}
is one dimensional.
Due to the specific form of
\mathbb{B}
is is obvious that the vectors (
1\leq p<q\leq n-1
)

\displaystyle V_{pq}=\begin{pmatrix}0\\
\vdots\\
0\\
\bar{\cal I}^{q}\\
0\\
\vdots\\
0\\
-\bar{\cal I}^{p}\\
0\\
\vdots\\
0\end{pmatrix}

where
\bar{\cal I}^{q}
is in the
p-
th line and
-\bar{\cal I}^{p}
in the
q-
th line are in the kernel of
\mathbb{B}
so the not normalised and not orthogonal vectors

\displaystyle T_{pp+1}=\bar{\cal I}^{p+1}S^{p}-\bar{\cal I}^{p}S^{p+1}\ ,\ \ p=1,\cdots,n-1

constitute a basis of the the kernel of
\mathbb{B}
and consequently are eigenvectors of
\mathbb{M}^{2}
with eigenvalue
a
.
Next it is easy to see that the vector

\displaystyle V=\begin{pmatrix}{\cal I}_{1}\\
\vdots\\
{\cal I}_{n}\end{pmatrix}

(F.1)

is a eigenvector of
\mathbb{B}
with eigenvalue
b|I|^{2}
. Thus the vector

\displaystyle S=\frac{{\cal I}_{p}}{|{\cal I}|}S^{p}\ ,

(F.2)

is a normalised eigenvector of
\mathbb{B}
. Note that
S
and
V_{ab}
are orthogonal.

To proceed to a change of basis we proceed in two steps. First we go from the canonical basis to the basis
V_{12},V_{21},\cdots,V_{n-1,n},S
. Denote
P
the corresponding matrix which takes the
form (in the
S^{p}-z
space)

\displaystyle P=\begin{pmatrix}\phantom{-}\bar{\cal I}^{2}&0&0&\cdots&0&\frac{{\cal I}_{1}}{|{\cal I}|}&0\\
-\bar{\cal I}^{1}&\phantom{-}\bar{\cal I}^{3}&0&\cdots&0&\frac{{\cal I}_{2}}{|{\cal I}|}&0\\
0&-\bar{\cal I}^{2}&\bar{\cal I}^{4}&\cdots&0&\frac{{\cal I}_{3}}{|{\cal I}|}&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&&\ddots&\vdots&\vdots&\vdots\\
0&0&0&\ddots&\bar{\cal I}^{n}&\frac{{\cal I}_{n-1}}{|{\cal I}|}&0\\
0&0&0&0&-\bar{\cal I}^{n-1}&\frac{{\cal I}_{n}}{|{\cal I}|}&0\\
0&&\cdots&&\cdots&0&1\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}Q&0\\
0&1\end{pmatrix}

where the matrix
Q
acts only on the
S-
sector and not on the hidden sector
z
.
Since the vectors
V_{12},\cdots,V_{n-1,n}
are not orthonormal, the matrix
P
(or
Q
) is not unitary.
Next we introduce a Gram Schmidt matrix
G
(acting only on the
S-
space) on the form

\displaystyle G=\begin{pmatrix}H&0\\
0&1\end{pmatrix}

which only acts on the
V_{12},\cdots,V_{n-1n}
vectors in such a way that they form an orthonormal basis. Since the vector
V
is normalised and orthogonal to all
V_{ab}
the matrix

G
only acts on the first
(n-1)-
components, say
V_{12},\cdots,V_{n,n-1}
and
enables to obtain an orthormal basis of the kernel of
\mathbb{B}
. We thus have

\displaystyle U=GQ=QG^{\prime}\ \ \text{with}\ \ G^{\prime}=Q^{-1}GQ

where now
U
is unitary. Thus we have in the new basis

\displaystyle\mathbb{A}^{\prime}=\begin{pmatrix}U^{\dagger}&0\\
0&1\end{pmatrix}\mathbb{A}\begin{pmatrix}U&0\\
0&1\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}U^{\dagger}&0\\
0&1\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\mathbb{B}&\mathbb{C}^{\dagger}\\
\mathbb{C}&\mathbb{D}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}U&0\\
0&1\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}U^{\dagger}\mathbb{B}U&U^{\dagger}\mathbb{C}\\
C^{\dagger}U&\mathbb{D}\end{pmatrix}

Of course we have

\displaystyle V^{\dagger}\mathbb{B}V=\text{diag}(0,\cdots,0,c|I|^{2})\ .

Furthermore, since

\displaystyle Q^{\dagger}C=\begin{pmatrix}0\\
\vdots\\
0\\
c|{\cal I}|\end{pmatrix}

and since the matrix
G^{\prime}
acts not trivially on the first
n-1
components we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}^{\prime 2}=\begin{pmatrix}a\mathbb{\cal I}_{n-1}&0&0\\
0&a+b|{\cal I}|^{2}&c|I|\\
0&\bar{c}|{\cal I}|&d\\
\end{pmatrix}

(F.3)

We now compute the orthornormal vectors of
\mathbb{B}
obtained by the Gram-Schmidt process.
The computation of the orthonormal basis is done by induction.
We start with the non-orthonormal basis of the kernel of
\mathbb{B}
that we denote

{\cal B}=\{V_{12},\cdots,V_{n-1,n}\}
. We denote
{\cal B}_{N}=\{W_{1},\cdots,W_{n-1}\}
the orthonormal (not
normalised) vecteurs. Pay attention that the orthonormalisation processe is done with the hermitian scalar
product. For convenience we introduce the following notations:

\displaystyle{\cal I}_{12}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|{\cal I}_{1}|^{2}+|{\cal I}_{2}|^{2}\

1.

Step one take

\displaystyle W_{1}=\Big(\frac{\bar{\cal I}^{2}}{{\cal I}_{12}},-\frac{\bar{\cal I}^{1}}{{\cal I}_{12}},0,\cdots,0\Big)^{t}\ .

2.

Step two take
W_{2}=W_{1}+\lambda V_{23}
imposing the
W_{1}^{\dagger}W_{2}=0
gives
\lambda
. Rescaling
W_{2}
leads to

\displaystyle W_{2}=\Big(\frac{{\cal I}_{1}}{{\cal I}_{12}},\frac{{\cal I}_{2}}{{\cal I}_{12}},-\frac{1}{\bar{\cal I}^{3}},\cdots,0\Big)^{t}\ .

3.

Step three. Since
V_{34}\perp W_{1}
, take
W_{3}=W_{3}+\lambda V_{34}
imposing the
W_{2}^{\dagger}W_{3}=0
gives
\lambda
. Rescaling
W_{3}
leads to

\displaystyle W_{3}=\Big(\frac{{\cal I}_{1}}{{\cal I}_{12}},\frac{{\cal I}_{2}}{{\cal I}_{12}},\frac{{\cal I}_{3}}{{\cal I}_{12}},-\frac{1}{{\cal I}_{12}\bar{{\cal I}}^{4}}\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{3}|{\cal I}_{p}|^{2},\cdots,0\Big)^{t}\ .

4.

Final steps. The relation above can be easily extended and proceeding along the same lines we get for
3\leq k\leq n-1

\displaystyle W_{k}=\Big(\frac{{\cal I}_{1}}{{\cal I}_{12}},\frac{{\cal I}_{2}}{{\cal I}_{12}},\frac{{\cal I}_{3}}{{\cal I}_{12}},\frac{{\cal I}_{4}}{{\cal I}_{12}},\cdots,\frac{{\cal I}_{k}}{{\cal I}_{12}},-\frac{1}{\bar{\cal I}^{k+1}{\cal I}_{12}}\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{k}|{\cal I}_{p}|^{2},\cdots,0\Big)^{t}\ .

Finally the vectors
(W_{1},\cdots,W_{n-1})
are orthonormal. A nice check that these vectors belongs to the kernel of
\mathbb{B}

is to check that these vectors are orthogonal to the eigenvectors
V
(see Eq[
F.1
]). And it is indeed the case.
The next step is to normalise the vectors
W_{k}
:

\displaystyle\|W_{1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\cal I}_{12}}

\displaystyle\|W_{2}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{3}|{\cal I}_{p}|^{2}}{{\cal I}_{12}|{\cal I}_{3}|^{2}}

\displaystyle\|W_{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{({\cal I}_{12})^{2}}\displaystyle\sum^{k}_{p=1}|{\cal I}_{p}|^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{|{\cal I}_{k+1}|^{2}}\displaystyle\sum^{k}_{p=1}|{\cal I}_{p}|^{2}\right),\quad 3\leq k\leq n-1

the components of the transerse impulsion of the particle and
(x,y)
the position of the displaced vertex. It is a parameter of interest for the study of displaced processes.
